default_locale = en

en.application.name = model_runner
en.application.summary = Local GGUF language-model execution. Loads a model file, tokenizes a prompt, evaluates the model on the CPU and streams the generated text. Inference is local only.
en.application.version = model_runner {version}
en.application.license = license {license}
en.application.architecture = architecture {name}
en.application.formats = tensor formats {value}
en.application.backends = backends {value}
en.application.chat_formats = chat formats {value}
en.application.devices = devices on this machine {value}

en.cli.label.standard_input = standard input

en.cli.general.usage = Usage: model_runner COMMAND [options]
en.cli.general.commands = Commands:
en.cli.general.command.run =   run MODEL       generate text from a model file
en.cli.general.command.embed =   embed MODEL     reduce a text to one vector
en.cli.general.command.inspect =   inspect MODEL   report what a model file contains
en.cli.general.command.models =   models [remove NAME]  list the models on hand, or remove one
en.cli.general.command.init =   init [TEMPLATE]  start a project's state from an installed template
en.cli.general.command.task =   task [ACTION]    list and manage the project's tasks
en.cli.general.command.repo =   repo [ACTION]    the repository's files, symbols and dependencies
en.cli.general.command.work =   work [TASK]      run a ready task from context to completion
en.cli.general.command.project =   req|state|bootstrap|config|reconfigure|check|...  a session's project command, from the shell
en.cli.general.command.help =   help [COMMAND]  show help for a command
en.cli.general.command.version =   version         show version information
en.cli.general.more = Run 'model_runner help COMMAND' for the options of a command.
en.cli.general.exit_statuses = Exit statuses: 0 success, 2 usage error, 3 model format error, 4 unsupported model feature, 5 resource failure, 6 input or output failure, 7 cancelled, 8 internal failure.

en.help.run.usage = Usage: model_runner run MODEL [options]
en.help.run.summary = Generate text from a local GGUF model file. A name with no path is read from the current directory, then from the models directory (MODEL_RUNNER_MODELS, or ~/.local/share/model_runner/models).
en.help.run.options = Options:
en.help.run.prompt =   --prompt TEXT           prompt text; repeatable, one sequence each
en.help.run.prompt_file =   --prompt-file PATH      read the prompt from a file
en.help.run.prompt_parts =   --prompt-parts JSON     the prompt as a list of parts: words, pictures and videos
en.help.run.interactive =   --interactive           hold a conversation on the terminal
en.help.run.agent =   --agent                 run the built-in tools and feed the answers back until the model answers
en.help.run.max_steps =   --max-steps N           most model turns the agent loop takes before giving up (default 8)
en.help.run.max_retries =   --max-retries N         retry a generation that errors this many times before giving up (default 0)
en.help.run.max_total_tokens =   --max-total-tokens N    stop the agent loop once it has generated this many tokens (default 0, no limit)
en.help.run.max_parallel =   --max-parallel N        run up to this many of a turn's parallel-safe calls at once (default 1)
en.help.run.confirm_tools =   --confirm-tools         ask on standard input before each tool call: y runs it, n declines it, q stops
en.help.run.deny_tool =   --deny-tool NAME        refuse every call to this tool (repeatable), for an unattended run
en.help.run.deny_arg =   --deny-arg TEXT         refuse any call whose arguments contain this text (repeatable)
en.help.run.compact =   --compact               drop the oldest turns to keep going when the conversation grows too large to render
en.help.run.raw =   --raw                   send the prompt unchanged, bypassing the chat template
en.help.run.think =   --think                 ask a model that reasons before it answers to do so
en.help.run.no_think =   --no-think              ask it not to; saying neither leaves the model as trained
en.help.run.load_session =   --load-session PATH     fill the context from a saved session; a bare name is found in the sessions directory
en.help.run.save_session =   --save-session PATH     write the context out at the end; a bare name is kept in the sessions directory
en.help.run.lora =   --lora PATH             merge a low-rank adapter; repeatable, they stack
en.help.run.lora_scale =   --lora-scale N          multiply that adapter's difference by this; -1 takes it off
en.help.run.json_schema =   --json-schema TEXT      constrain the output to a JSON schema
en.help.run.json_schema_file =   --json-schema-file PATH read that schema from a file
en.help.run.grammar =   --grammar TEXT          constrain the output to a grammar
en.help.run.grammar_file =   --grammar-file PATH     read that grammar from a file
en.help.run.tools =   --tools JSON            offer the model these tools, as JSON
en.help.run.tools_file =   --tools-file PATH       read those definitions from a file
en.help.run.tool_command =   --tool-command CMD      run each tool call through this program in place of the built-in tools
en.help.run.assistant =   --assistant TEXT        a reply the model gave earlier, as a turn of the conversation
en.help.run.assistant_file =   --assistant-file PATH   read that reply from a file
en.help.run.tool_result =   --tool-result TEXT      what a tool answered, as a turn of its own; repeatable
en.help.run.tool_result_file =   --tool-result-file PATH read that answer from a file
en.help.run.assistant_parts =   --assistant-parts JSON  a reply the model gave earlier, as a list of parts
en.help.run.tool_result_parts =   --tool-result-parts JSON what a tool answered, as a list of parts
en.help.run.repack =   --repack MODE           rewrite the weights once at load: {value}
en.help.run.kv_cache =   --kv-cache MODE         how the context is stored: {value}
en.help.run.kv_values =   --kv-values MODE        how the values are stored where it differs from the keys: {value}, beside a packed --kv-cache
en.help.run.paged =   --paged                 deal the device's cache in pages, paying for the positions filled; on by default
en.help.run.no_paged =   --no-paged              reserve the whole context as one block instead
en.help.run.no_cache =   --no-cache              reuse and rewrite no prefill cache; every prompt is read in full
en.help.run.arith =   --arith MODE            how a matrix product multiplies: {value}
en.help.run.system =   --system TEXT           system message
en.help.run.system_file =   --system-file PATH      read the system message from a file
en.help.run.developer =   --developer TEXT        developer message, after the system message
en.help.run.max_tokens =   --max-tokens N          largest number of tokens to generate; 0 reads the prompt only
en.help.run.context_size =   --context-size N        context capacity in tokens
en.help.run.rope_scaling =   --rope-scaling NAME     stretch the rotation: none, linear, yarn
en.help.run.rope_scale =   --rope-scale N          stretch it by this; 2 is twice the context
en.help.run.rope_freq_base =   --rope-freq-base N      the angle the first pair turns by
en.help.run.yarn_orig_context =   --yarn-orig-context N   the context the model was trained on
en.help.run.yarn_attn_factor =   --yarn-attn-factor N    what yarn attenuates the whole by
en.help.run.yarn_beta_fast =   --yarn-beta-fast N      the fast end of the band yarn mixes across
en.help.run.yarn_beta_slow =   --yarn-beta-slow N      the slow end of it
en.help.run.threads =   --threads N             worker tasks for matrix products; 0 chooses, 1 is serial
en.help.run.backend =   --backend NAME          backend to evaluate the model on: {value}
en.help.run.batch_size =   --batch-size N          prompt tokens evaluated per pass over the weights
en.help.run.temperature =   --temperature X         sampling temperature; 0 selects greedily
en.help.run.top_k =   --top-k N               keep the N highest-scoring candidates; 0 disables
en.help.run.top_p =   --top-p X               keep the smallest set whose probability reaches X
en.help.run.min_p =   --min-p X               drop candidates below X times the most probable
en.help.run.top_a =   --top-a X               drop candidates below X times the square of the most probable
en.help.run.typical =   --typical X             keep the candidates least surprising to the model; 1 disables
en.help.run.tail_free =   --tail-free X           cut where the sorted curve stops falling; 1 disables
en.help.run.xtc_probability =   --xtc-probability X     chance of dropping the likeliest candidates; 0 disables
en.help.run.xtc_threshold =   --xtc-threshold X       how probable a candidate must be for that to reach it
en.help.run.dry_multiplier =   --dry-multiplier X      penalty for continuing a repeated sequence; 0 disables
en.help.run.dry_base =   --dry-base X            raised to how far the repetition runs past what is allowed
en.help.run.dry_allowed_length =   --dry-allowed-length N  how long a repeat may be before it is penalized
en.help.run.mirostat =   --mirostat N            steer for a steady surprise; 0 disables, 2 is the version
en.help.run.mirostat_tau =   --mirostat-tau X        the surprise, in bits, to steer towards
en.help.run.mirostat_eta =   --mirostat-eta X        how fast to steer
en.help.run.chat_template =   --chat-template NAME    use a chat format this build carries: {value}
en.help.run.chat_template_file =   --chat-template-file PATH  read a chat template from a file
en.help.run.delegate_system_file =   --delegate-system-file PATH  read the sub-agent system prompt from a file
en.help.run.repeat_penalty =   --repeat-penalty X      penalty for recently produced tokens; 1 disables
en.help.run.frequency_penalty =   --frequency-penalty X   subtracted once per repetition of a token; 0 disables
en.help.run.presence_penalty =   --presence-penalty X    subtracted from any token already produced; 0 disables
en.help.run.repeat_window =   --repeat-window N       how many recent tokens the penalty considers
en.help.run.seed =   --seed N                fixed 64-bit seed
en.help.run.stop =   --stop TEXT             stop when this text is produced; repeatable
en.help.run.stop_token =   --stop-token N          stop on this token identifier; repeatable
en.help.run.draft_model =   --draft-model PATH      a smaller model to propose tokens for this one to check
en.help.run.mmproj =   --mmproj PATH           the vision projector a picture or a video in --prompt-parts is read with
en.help.run.pan_and_scan =   --pan-and-scan          show a wide or tall picture whole and then in crops, as the reference can
en.help.run.embed_model =   --embed-model PATH      a model the agent's retrieve tool embeds with, to rank a folder by meaning
en.help.run.memory_file =   --memory-file PATH      keep the agent's memory in this file, so it outlives the run
en.help.run.trace_file =   --trace-file PATH       write a JSON trace of the agent run -- its calls, results and timings -- to this file
en.help.run.checkpoint_file =   --checkpoint-file PATH  resume the agent run from this file if it holds a conversation, and checkpoint to it at the end
en.help.run.draft_tokens =   --draft-tokens N        how many it may propose at a time (4); a model with a next-token block drafts from it unasked, and 0 turns that off
en.help.run.draft_lookup =   --draft-lookup          propose them from the context instead, with no draft model
en.help.run.context_shift =   --context-shift N       when the context fills, drop the oldest N and carry on
en.help.run.context_keep =   --context-keep N        how many at the front to keep when that happens
en.help.run.logprobs =   --logprobs N            report each token's log-probability and the N likeliest
en.help.run.logit_bias =   --logit-bias TOKEN=X    add X to that token's logit; repeatable
en.help.run.memory_limit =   --memory-limit SIZE     limit model memory; accepts K, M and G suffixes
en.help.run.device_memory =   --device-memory SIZE    device memory for the weights; 0 reads them where they lie
en.help.run.device_patience =   --device-patience N     seconds to wait for one product before giving up on the device
en.help.run.device =   --device N              which of the host's devices to compute on; inspect lists them
en.help.run.mmap =   --mmap                  require memory mapping of the model file
en.help.run.no_mmap =   --no-mmap               never memory-map the model file
en.help.run.quiet =   --quiet                 suppress progress, notes and warnings
en.help.run.verbose =   --verbose               report progress and detail on standard error
en.help.run.show_stats =   --show-stats            report generation statistics
en.help.run.no_stats =   --no-stats              do not report generation statistics
en.help.run.locale =   --locale NAME           locale for program messages
en.help.run.color =   --color=MODE            colour output: {value}
en.help.run.streams = Generated text goes to standard output. Diagnostics, progress and statistics go to standard error.
en.help.run.privacy = A prompt given with --prompt may be visible to other local processes. Use --prompt-file or standard input for sensitive text.

en.help.inspect.usage = Usage: model_runner inspect MODEL [options]
en.help.inspect.summary = Parse and validate a model file and report what it contains, without generating anything.
en.help.models.usage = Usage: model_runner models [remove NAME]
en.help.models.summary = List the models in the models directory with their sizes, or remove one by name (with all its shards). The directory is MODEL_RUNNER_MODELS, or ~/.local/share/model_runner/models.

en.help.init.usage = Usage: model_runner init [TEMPLATE] [options]
en.help.init.summary = Start the project state of spec-driven development in a directory, from an installed project template. Without a template named, a terminal is offered the templates to choose from, and anything else is shown them and told to name one. Inputs the template asks for are taken from --set, found in the project, or defaulted; a terminal is asked for the rest. Templates are read from templates beside the settings file and from the templates installed with the program.
en.help.init.options = Options:
en.help.init.set =   --set NAME=VALUE        give the template's input NAME its value; repeatable
en.help.init.directory =   --directory DIR         initialize DIR rather than the current directory
en.help.init.format =   --format plain|json     json: one object a line, each message's key and values, for a program
en.help.init.quiet =   --quiet                 suppress progress, notes and warnings
en.help.init.verbose =   --verbose               report progress and detail on standard error
en.help.init.locale =   --locale NAME           locale for program messages
en.help.init.color =   --color=MODE            colour output: {value}
en.help.task.usage = Usage: model_runner task [list|new TITLE|accept ID|reject ID|cancel ID|reopen ID|reconsider ID|move ID STATE|audit ID|edit ID|depend ID ON|split ID TITLE; TITLE|show ID|context ID|verify ID|complete ID|integrate ID [anyway]|derive|step|plan] [options]
en.help.task.summary = Manage the project's persistent tasks. list shows each with its state, ready shown as one; new creates a candidate from --set fields, asking a terminal for what its kind requires; accept, reject and cancel move one; show prints the effective task; derive makes the tasks accepted requirements imply. Tasks that became ready are named after each change.
en.help.task.options = Options:
en.help.task.set =   --set NAME=VALUE        give the new task's field NAME its value; repeatable
en.help.task.directory =   --directory DIR         the project in DIR rather than the current directory

en.help.repo.usage = Usage: model_runner repo [scan|tree|sym NAME|refs NAME|deps UNIT|users UNIT|impact FILE|trace NODE] [options]
en.help.repo.summary = Scan the project's files without a model and answer from what was found: scan says how much, tree lists the files with their language and role, sym finds a declared symbol, refs lists where it is used, deps and users follow the with clauses. In a project with state, the graph is kept in its indexes.
en.help.repo.options = Options:

en.help.work.usage = Usage: model_runner work [TASK] [options]
en.help.work.summary = Run one ready task: an agent holds it in a new generation, its context is built and kept, the model call recorded, and what the agent answers is held to the work contract. The files show what changed; the task's verification runs, and it completes through its gates or ends blocked or failed with why. The agent is --set model=PATH, run as this program's agent with no shell, network or delegation, or the configuration's scalar work.agent command, with $PROMPT for its context's file. Without a task named, a terminal chooses one.
en.help.work.options = Options:
en.help.work.set =   --set NAME=VALUE        model=PATH, steps=N or profile=ID or all=yes; repeatable
en.help.work.directory =   --directory DIR         the project in DIR rather than the current directory
en.help.work.format =   --format plain|json     json: one object a line, each message's key and values, for a program
en.help.work.quiet =   --quiet                 suppress progress, notes and warnings
en.help.work.verbose =   --verbose               report progress and detail on standard error
en.help.work.locale =   --locale NAME           locale for program messages
en.help.work.color =   --color=MODE            colour output: {value}
en.help.project.usage = Usage: model_runner req|state|bootstrap|config|reconfigure|check|decision|spec|result|sandbox|instruct|accept|reject [WORDS] [options]
en.help.project.summary = Run a project command without a model, as a session's /COMMAND runs it: req new, show, accept, revise and link requirements; state reports the project; bootstrap reads documents in; config shows the settings and reconfigure changes them (NAME=VALUE, confirm=yes off a terminal; set.NAME+=VALUE adds to a set); check verifies; result shows a stored result. Work is the work command's.
en.help.project.req = req [list] ; req new TITLE text=... [criteria=...] ; req ID ; req accept, reject or obsolete ID ; req revise ID title=... text=... criteria=... ; req link ID KIND TARGET and req unlink ID KIND TARGET, KIND one of dependency, component, implementation, task, test, verification ; req supersede OLD NEW
en.help.project.state = state: the project at a glance -- its requirements and tasks counted, the blocked and failed ones with why, the last verification
en.help.project.bootstrap = bootstrap [FILE ...]: read the project's documents for its requirements (## REQ-ID Title headings, REQ-ID: lines, SHALL, MUST and SHOULD lines, Acceptance: and DEC-ID: lines)
en.help.project.config = config [NAME]: the settings, or those whose name holds NAME
en.help.project.reconfigure = reconfigure NAME=VALUE ... [confirm=yes]: change settings -- NAME with its kind or without (work.agent), set.NAME+=A,B adds to a set and -=A takes out, map.permission.X= grants and =off takes away; confirm=yes applies without being asked
en.help.project.check = check [PROFILE, full, consistency or REQ-ID]: run the project's checks, or say what does not hold together, or verify a requirement
en.help.project.decision = decision [list] ; decision new TITLE text=... ; decision ID ; decision accept, reject or obsolete ID ; decision revise ID text=... ; decision govern ID SETTING RULING
en.help.project.spec = spec [list] ; spec new TITLE text=... ; spec ID ; spec accept or reject ID ; spec revise ID text=... ; spec revise ID from-document takes what its document says now
en.help.project.accept = accept [ID]: accept the one candidate waiting, or the requirement, decision, specification or task named
en.help.project.reject = reject [ID]: reject the one candidate waiting, or the one named
en.help.project.sandbox = sandbox [CAPABILITY: roots=...; ... or off]: confine the session's agents below the project's permissions; from the shell, MODEL_RUNNER_SANDBOX does it for one command
en.help.project.instruct = instruct TEXT: an instruction every agent of the project is given from now on
en.help.project.result = result ID [full]: what a result, invocation, context, evidence or agent (RES-, INV-, CTX-, VER-, AG-) holds
en.help.project.options = Options:
en.help.project.directory =   --directory DIR         the project in DIR rather than the current directory
en.help.project.format =   --format plain|json     json: one object a line, each message's key and values, for a program
en.help.project.quiet =   --quiet                 suppress progress, notes and warnings
en.help.project.verbose =   --verbose               report progress and detail on standard error
en.help.project.locale =   --locale NAME           locale for program messages

en.help.embed.usage = Usage: model_runner embed MODEL [options]
en.help.embed.summary = Reduce a text to one vector: what the model made of it, before the projection that turns a state into a distribution over tokens. The prompt is read as written; no chat template is applied.
en.help.embed.options = Options:
en.help.embed.prompt =   --prompt TEXT           text to embed
en.help.embed.prompt_file =   --prompt-file PATH      read the text from a file
en.help.embed.pooling =   --pooling KIND          how positions are reduced: {value}
en.help.embed.no_normalize =   --no-normalize          report the vector as computed, not at unit length
en.help.embed.query =   --query TEXT            rerank: score each --prompt line against this query, one score a line
en.help.embed.batch_size =   --batch-size N          tokens evaluated in one pass over the weights
en.help.embed.threads =   --threads N             worker tasks; 0 chooses
en.help.embed.backend =   --backend NAME          backend to run on: {value}
en.help.embed.repack =   --repack MODE           rewrite the weights once at load: {value}
en.help.embed.kv_cache =   --kv-cache MODE         how the context is stored: {value}
en.help.embed.kv_values =   --kv-values MODE        how the values are stored where it differs from the keys: {value}, beside a packed --kv-cache
en.help.embed.arith =   --arith MODE            how a matrix product multiplies: {value}
en.help.embed.context_size =   --context-size N        context capacity in tokens; 0 uses the model's
en.help.embed.memory_limit =   --memory-limit BYTES    refuse a model that would need more
en.help.embed.mmap =   --mmap=MODE             map the file instead of reading it
en.help.embed.quiet =   --quiet                 suppress progress, notes and warnings
en.help.embed.verbose =   --verbose               report progress and detail on standard error
en.help.embed.locale =   --locale NAME           locale for program messages
en.help.embed.color =   --color=MODE            colour output: {value}
en.help.embed.streams = The vector goes to standard output, one component a line. Diagnostics go to standard error.
en.help.embed.rope_scaling =   --rope-scaling NAME     stretch the rotation: none, linear, yarn
en.help.embed.rope_scale =   --rope-scale N          stretch it by this; 2 is twice the context
en.help.embed.rope_freq_base =   --rope-freq-base N      the angle the first pair turns by
en.help.embed.yarn_orig_context =   --yarn-orig-context N   the context the model was trained on
en.help.embed.yarn_attn_factor =   --yarn-attn-factor N    what yarn attenuates the whole by
en.help.embed.yarn_beta_fast =   --yarn-beta-fast N      the fast end of the band yarn mixes across
en.help.embed.yarn_beta_slow =   --yarn-beta-slow N      the slow end of it
en.help.embed.device_memory =   --device-memory SIZE    device memory for the weights; 0 reads them where they lie
en.help.embed.device_patience =   --device-patience N     seconds to wait for one product before giving up on the device
en.help.embed.device =   --device N              which of the host's devices to compute on; inspect lists them

# Danish. A partial translation: keys that are absent here fall back
# to the default locale per key, so untranslated messages still render.
da.application.name = model_runner
da.application.summary = Lokal kørsel af GGUF-sprogmodeller. Indlæser en modelfil, opdeler en prompt i tokens, evaluerer modellen på CPU'en og streamer den genererede tekst. Al inferens sker lokalt.
da.application.version = model_runner {version}
da.application.license = licens {license}
da.application.architecture = arkitektur {name}
da.application.formats = tensorformater {value}
da.application.backends = backends {value}
da.application.devices = enheder på denne maskine {value}
da.application.chat_formats = chatformater {value}
da.cli.label.standard_input = standardinddata

da.cli.general.usage = Brug: model_runner KOMMANDO [tilvalg]
da.cli.general.commands = Kommandoer:
da.cli.general.command.run = run MODEL       generér tekst ud fra en modelfil
da.cli.general.command.embed =   embed MODEL     reducér en tekst til én vektor
da.cli.general.command.inspect = inspect MODEL   vis hvad en modelfil indeholder
da.cli.general.command.models =   models [remove NAME]  vis modellerne ved hånden, eller fjern en
da.cli.general.command.init =   init [SKABELON]  start et projekts tilstand fra en installeret skabelon
da.cli.general.command.task =   task [HANDLING]  vis og styr projektets opgaver
da.cli.general.command.repo =   repo [HANDLING]  depotets filer, symboler og afhængigheder
da.cli.general.command.work =   work [OPGAVE]    kør en klar opgave fra kontekst til fuldførelse
da.cli.general.command.project =   req|state|bootstrap|config|reconfigure|check|...  en sessions projektkommando, fra skallen
da.cli.general.command.help = help [KOMMANDO] vis hjælp til en kommando
da.cli.general.command.version = version         vis versionsoplysninger
da.cli.general.more = Kør 'model_runner help KOMMANDO' for at se en kommandos tilvalg.
da.cli.general.exit_statuses = Slutstatus: 0 klaret, 2 fejl i kommandolinjen, 3 fejl i modelformatet, 4 modelfunktion understøttes ikke, 5 ressourcefejl, 6 ind- eller uddatafejl, 7 afbrudt, 8 intern fejl.
